But here's the critical issue: the server also sets `socket->setProperty("role","device")` for each device.register message. If a charger code doesn't exist in the central database (`updateHeartbeat` returns false), that code is skipped, but the OTHER codes on the same socket are still registered.

Wait, let me re-read the device.register handler:


The server validates the token, then iterates through each charger code in the registration message, checking if it exists in the database via `updateHeartbeat` — if the code is invalid, it skips that charger and continues to the next one.

After processing all codes, it stores the socket-to-charger mapping and returns the count of successfully registered chargers. I'm noticing a potential issue though: if `updateHeartbeat` fails because a charger code doesn't exist in the database, that code gets skipped silently without any error being raised, which could cause the second charger to fail registration without the user knowing why.

The user's message suggests they're only running the simulator with codes for the first charging station, not the second one. The "未连接" status they're seeing might be the default offline state in the admin client, or it could be indicating that the second station's chargers aren't being simulated at all. Looking at the database schema, chargers have a status field that can be IDLE, CHARGING, FAULT, or OFFLINE, and this status gets updated when the device sends heartbeats. If the simulator isn't running for a particular station's chargers, those chargers would remain in whatever state they were last in.

The real question is what the user is actually passing as command-line arguments to the simulator. If they're running it with codes for both stations but only one shows as connected, there's likely a registration or heartbeat issue preventing the second station's chargers from being properly recognized by the server. I'm realizing the user might be describing a different scenario—maybe they're running the simulator with codes for both stations, but only one charger per station is actually working. Let me trace through the device registration logic to see if there's something preventing the second charger from registering properly. Or more likely, the user is running a single simulator with all three charger codes, but the server's registration loop is silently skipping a charger if `updateHeartbeat` fails—meaning only the first charger would actually be active in the system.

## Assistant (Plan · MiMo V2.5 Free · 22.5s)

_Thinking:_

The user is running the simulator directly from Qt Creator without passing any parameters. That means it uses the default value: `--code DL-SW-001`.

So the simulator only registers ONE charger code: `DL-SW-001` (station 1). The other chargers (DL-SW-002 at station 1, and DL-GX-002 at station 2) are not connected to any device.

That's why station 2 (高新园区充电站) shows "未连接" - there's no simulator running for its charger code `DL-GX-001`.


The real issue is that the simulator needs to be configured with all three charger codes to cover both stations, or the user needs to launch multiple simulator instances with different codes. Let me also check if there are other issues in the simulator code itself, particularly around how it handles multiple charger codes.

Looking at the server's response to the device registration, I can see it returns the count of registered chargers. The simulator doesn't seem to validate this response or log the registered codes, which could be masking problems if some chargers fail to register. This might be worth improving for better debugging visibility.

The real issue is clear though: the simulator was launched with just one charger code by default, so only that charger connects. The fix would be to either configure the default to include all charger codes or update the simulator to handle multiple codes properly.

明白了！原因找到了。你直接在 Creator 里启动 simulator，**没有传参数**，所以它使用默认值 `--code DL-SW-001`，只模拟了站1的第一个充电桩。站2的 `DL-GX-001` 和站1的 `DL-SW-002` 都没有设备模拟器连接。

## 根因

`device_simulator/main.cpp:13` 默认值只设了一个充电桩：
```cpp
p.addOption({"code","Comma-separated charger codes","codes","DL-SW-001"});
```

所以只注册了 `DL-SW-001` 一个充电桩，其他充电桩没有模拟器连接服务器。

## 修复方案

**改默认值**：把 simulator 的 `--code` 默认值改为包含所有三个充电桩代码：

```cpp
p.addOption({"code","Comma-separated charger codes","codes","DL-SW-001,DL-SW-002,DL-GX-001"});
```

这样直接在 Creator 里启动时，三个充电桩都会注册到服务器，两个充电站都会显示已连接。

| 文件 | 改动 |
|---|---|
| `device_simulator/main.cpp:13` | `--code` 默认值从 `DL-SW-001` 改为 `DL-SW-001,DL-SW-002,DL-GX-001` |
